# Experience Heritage Living In A Shophouse Boutique Hotel and Serviced Apartment

Located within Singapore's cultural districts, we are the largest provider of short term and long term accommodation in these neighbourhoods. Our [boutique hotels](https://hericoll.com/blog/best-boutique-hotels-in-singapore/) and serviced apartments are stocked with the essentials needed for your stay, and you're always steps away from the best local food haunts and cultural attractions.

Choose your preferred location based on our Neighbourhoods, or browse by Room type.

## Our rooms, your haven

Heritage Collection is your gateway to Singapore’s cultural neighbourhoods and each has its own unique story to tell.

We will take you to a liminal space of the old and new and help you discover Singapore through the footsteps of a local.

Inquisitive cultural enthusiasts of the world, get ready for your senses to be awakened!

[EXPLORE OUR ROOMS](https://hericoll.com/collections/)

# Corporate Bookings

![Corporate Bookings](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/Corporate%20Bookings%20Page%20Header.jpg)

## Are you a frequent  Business traveller or Corporate travel booker?

### If yes, a good deal is waiting for you!

With properties in unbeatable locations within the Singapore Central Business District, Heritage Collection offers you and your guests the convenience of an easy booking process and fuss-free stay at a reasonable budget. Share with us your requirements, and we'll help you book the ideal accommodation.

## Other Benefits of a  Corporate Account

- Complimentary Limo Airport Transfer with Meet-and-Greet Service \*
- Complimentary Starbucks card \*\*
- Complimentary Transport credits \*\*
- Upgrade to bi-weekly housekeeping service \*\*
- Early check-in or late check-out \*\*\*
- Discounted rate for bulk bookings
- Exclusive seasonal discounts for Corporate Accounts

\\* Minimum booking of $1,900 for total stay

\\*\\* Dependent on volume of booking and duration of stay

\\*\\*\\* Subject to apartment availability

## Privacy Policy Overview
[Skip to content](https://hericoll.com/privacy-policy/#primary)

# Privacy Policy

![Fulfilling your inner wanderlust](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/Individual%20Promo%20Page%20Header.jpg)

This Privacy Policy (“Policy”) sets out the basis which HERITAGE COLLECTION PTE. LTD. and our subsidiaries (“we”, “us”, or “our”) may collect, use, disclose or otherwise process personal data of our customers in accordance with the Personal Data Protection Act 2012 (“PDPA”). This Policy applies to personal data in our possession or under our control, including personal data in the possession of organisations which we have engaged to collect, use, disclose or process personal data for our purposes.

###### **PERSONAL DATA**

1. As used in this Policy:

“customer” means an individual, company, organisation or entity who (a) has contacted us through any means to find out more about any goods or services we provide, or (b) may, or has, entered into a contract with us for the supply of any goods or services by us; and



“personal data” means data, whether true or not, about a customer who can be identified: (a) from that data; or (b) from that data and other information to which we have or are likely to have access.

2. Depending on the nature of your interaction with us, some examples of personal data which we may collect from you include name, identification numbers such as NRIC, FIN, work permit and birth certificate, residential address, email address, telephone number, nationality, gender, date of birth, financial information, passport information, photograph identification, credit card information and payment information.
3. Other terms used in this Policy shall have the meanings given to them in the PDPA (where the context so permits).

###### **COLLECTION, USE AND DISCLOSURE OF PERSONAL DATA**

1. We generally do not collect your personal data unless (a) it is provided to us voluntarily by you directly or via a third party who has been duly authorised by you to disclose your personal data to us (your “authorised representative”) after (i) you (or your authorised representative) have been notified of the purposes for which the data is collected, and (ii) you (or your authorised representative) have provided written consent to the collection and usage of your personal data for those purposes, or (b) collection and use of personal data without consent is permitted or required by the PDPA or other laws. We shall seek your consent before collecting any additional personal data and before using your personal data for a purpose which has not been notified to you (except where permitted or authorised by law).
2. We may collect and use your personal data for any or all of the following purposes:
1. performing obligations in the course of or in connection with our provision of the

      goods and/or services requested by you;
2. verifying your identity;
3. responding to, handling, and processing queries, requests, applications, complaints,

      and feedback from you;
4. managing your relationship with us;
5. processing payment or credit transactions;
6. complying with any applicable laws, regulations, codes of practice, guidelines, or

      rules, or to assist in law enforcement and investigations conducted by any

      governmental and/or regulatory authority;
7. any other purposes for which you have provided the information;
8. transmitting to any unaffiliated third parties including our third party service

      providers and agents, and relevant governmental and/or regulatory authorities,

      whether in Singapore or abroad, for the aforementioned purposes; and
9. any other incidental business purposes related to or in connection with the above.
3. We may disclose your personal data:
1. where such disclosure is required for performing obligations in the course of or in connection with our provision of the goods and services requested by you; or
2. to third party service providers, agents and other organisations we have engaged to perform any of the functions with reference to the above mentioned purposes.
4. The purposes listed in the above clauses may continue to apply even in situations where your relationship with us has been terminated or altered in any way, for a reasonable period thereafter (including, where applicable, a period to enable us to enforce our rights under a contract with you).

###### **WITHDRAWING YOUR CONSENT**

1. The consent that you provide for the collection, use and disclosure of your personal data will remain valid until such time it is being withdrawn by you in writing. You may withdraw consent and request us to stop collecting, using and/or disclosing your personal data for any or all of the purposes listed above by submitting your request in writing or via email to us at the contact details provided below.
2. Upon receipt of your written request to withdraw your consent, we may require reasonable time (depending on the complexity of the request and its impact on our relationship with you) for your request to be processed and for us to notify you of the consequences of us acceding to the same, including any legal consequences which may affect your rights and liabilities to us. In general, we shall seek to process your request within fourteen (14) business days of receiving it.
3. Whilst we respect your decision to withdraw your consent, please note that depending on the nature and scope of your request, we may not be in a position to continue providing our goods or services to you and we shall, in such circumstances, notify you before completing the processing of your request. Should you decide to cancel your withdrawal of consent, please inform us in writing in the manner described in clause 8 above.
4. Please note that withdrawing consent does not affect our right to continue to collect, use and disclose personal data where such collection, use and disclose without consent is permitted or required under applicable laws.

###### **ACCESS TO AND CORRECTION OF PERSONAL DATA**

1. If you wish to make (a) an access request for access to a copy of the personal data which we hold about you or information about the ways in which we use or disclose your personal data, or (b) a correction request to correct or update any of your personal data which we hold about you, you may submit your request in writing or via email to us at the contact details provided below.
2. Please note that a reasonable fee may be charged for an access request. If so, we will inform you of the fee before processing your request.
3. We will respond to your request as soon as reasonably possible. In general, our response will be within fourteen (14) business days. Should we not be able to respond to your request within thirty (30) days after receiving your request, we will inform you in writing within thirty (30) days of the time by which we will be able to respond to your request. If we are unable to provide you with any personal data or to make a correction requested by you, we shall generally inform you of the reasons why we are unable to do so (except where we are not required to do so under the PDPA).

###### **PROTECTION OF PERSONAL DATA**

1. To safeguard your personal data from unauthorised access, collection, use, disclosure, copying, modification, disposal or similar risks, we have introduced appropriate administrative, physical and technical measures such as encryption, use of privacy filters, and disclosing personal data both internally and to our authorised third party service providers and agents only on a need-to-know basis.
2. You should be aware, however, that no method of transmission over the Internet or method of electronic storage is completely secure. While security cannot be guaranteed, we strive to protect the security of your information and are constantly reviewing and enhancing our information security measures.

###### **RETENTION OF PERSONAL DATA**

1. We may retain your personal data for as long as it is necessary to fulfil the purpose for which it was collected, or as required or permitted by applicable laws.
2. We will cease to retain your personal data, or remove the means by which the data can be associated with you, as soon as it is reasonable to assume that such retention no longer serves the purpose for which the personal data was collected, and is no longer necessary for legal or business purposes.

###### **TRANSFERS OF PERSONAL DATA OUTSIDE OF SINGAPORE**

1. We generally do not transfer your personal data to countries outside of Singapore. However, if we do so, we will obtain your consent for the transfer to be made and we will take steps to ensure that your personal data continues to receive a standard of protection that is at least comparable to that provided under the PDPA.

###### **DATA PROTECTION POINT-OF-CONTACT**

1. You may contact us if you have any enquiries or feedback on our personal data protection policies and procedures, or if you wish to make any request, in the following manner:

Contact No.: +65 6223 7155

Email Address: sgenquiry@hericoll.com

###### **EFFECT OF POLICY AND CHANGES TO POLICY**

1. This Policy applies in conjunction with any other policies, notices, contractual clauses and consent clauses that apply in relation to the collection, use and disclosure of your personal data by us.
2. We may revise this Policy from time to time without any prior notice. You may determine if any such revision has taken place by referring to the date on which this Policy was last updated. Your continued use of our services constitutes your acknowledgement and acceptance of such changes.

# Terms Of Use

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/01/20211108_W9A0958-HDR-46_E02-48-4-scaled.jpg)

###### **TERMS OF USE**

Please read these terms of use (“terms of use”, “terms”) carefully before using hericoll.com (“website”, “service”) operated by Heritage Collection Pte Ltd (“us”, ‘we”, “our”).

###### **CONDITIONS OF USE**

By using this website, you certify that you have read and reviewed this Agreement and that you agree to comply with its terms. If you do not want to be bound by the terms of this Agreement, you are advised to leave the website accordingly. Heritage Collection only grants use and access of this website, its products, and its services to those who have accepted its terms.

###### **PRIVACY POLICY**

Before you continue using our website, we advise you to read our [privacy policy](https://hericoll.com/privacy-policy/) regarding our user data collection. It will help you better understand our practices.

###### **AGE RESTRICTION**

You must be at least 18 (eighteen) years of age before you can make a booking via this website. By using this website, you warrant that you are at least 18 years of age and you may legally adhere to this Agreement. Heritage Collection assumes no responsibility for liabilities related to age misrepresentation.

###### **INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**

You agree that all materials, products, and services provided on this website are the property of Heritage Collection, its affiliates, directors, officers, employees, agents, suppliers, or licensors including all copyrights, trade secrets, trademarks, patents, and other intellectual property. You also agree that you will not reproduce or redistribute Heritage Collection’s intellectual property in any way, including electronic, digital, or new trademark registrations.

You grant Heritage Collection a royalty-free and non-exclusive license to display, use, copy, transmit, and broadcast the content you upload and publish. For issues regarding intellectual property claims, you may contact us in order to come to an agreement.

###### **USER ACCOUNTS**

As a user of this website, you may be asked to register with us and provide private information. You are responsible for ensuring the accuracy of this information, and you are responsible for maintaining the safety and security of your identifying information. You are also responsible for all activities that occur under your account or password.

If you think there are any possible issues regarding the security of your account on the website, inform us immediately so we may address it accordingly.

We reserve all rights to terminate accounts, and cancel bookings in their sole discretion.

###### **APPLICABLE LAW**

By visiting this website, you agree that the laws of Singapore, without regard to principles of conflict laws, will govern these terms and conditions, or any dispute of any sort that might come between Heritage Collection and you, or its business partners and associates.

###### **DISPUTES**

Any dispute related in any way to your visit to this website or to bookings you made with us shall be arbitrated by state court in Singapore and you consent to exclusive jurisdiction and venue of such courts.

###### **INDEMNIFICATION**

You agree to indemnify Heritage Collection and its affiliates and hold Heritage Collection harmless against legal claims and demands that may arise from your use or misuse of our services. We reserve the right to select our own legal counsel.

###### **LIMITATION ON LIABILITY**

Heritage Collection is not liable for any damages that may occur to you as a result of your misuse of our website.

###### **EFFECT OF TERMS AND CHANGES TO TERMS**

Heritage Collection reserves the right to edit, modify, and change this Agreement any time without any prior notice. This Agreement is an understanding between Heritage Collection and the user, and this supersedes and replaces all prior agreements regarding the use of this website. Your continued use of the website and our services constitutes your acknowledgement and acceptance of such changes.

## Heritage NYC Apartments
[Skip to content](https://hericoll.com/heritage-nyc-no-fee-apartments/#primary)

# Experience NYC in Heritage Collection’s No Fee Apartments

![NYC No Fee Apartments - no broker fee and lower rent](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/NYC_GettyImages-640006562.jpeg)

Are you a student looking for unique and affordable lodging in New York City? Or a young professional looking for flexible leases while working in Manhattan? Or just landed your first summer internship in New York?

Heritage Collection New York is your gateway to city living. Every neighborhood is vibrant and full of life, and our apartments are where you get to experience the vivacious elements first-hand. Search through our apartment listings to find the unit you’re looking for. Our apartments range from a [Private Bedroom](https://hericoll.com/ny/apartments/) or [Studio 1 Single Apartment](https://hericoll.com/ny/apartments/) to a [Studio 1 2 Deluxe](https://hericoll.com/ny/apartments/) and 1 Bed or 2-Bedroom Apartment.

Our New York City apartments are conveniently located within popular New York neighborhoods, such as Greenwich, Chelsea, East Village and Grand Central.

Start your city adventures at the doorstep of your new home in our neighborhoods – right in the heart of Manhattan! Our no-fee apartments come fully furnished and are well located within the vibrant locations in Manhattan.

Contact us now to find out more details:

Email: [nyenquiry@hericoll.com](mailto:nyenquiry@hericoll.com)

Office: +1 (646) 484-6157

Mobile: +1 (917) 244-4541

## **We Offer NYC No-Fee Apartments for Rent**

If you’ve been on multiple apartment listing sites struggling to find a no-fee rental apartment in New York, you’ve come to the right place.

The New York City housing market can be incredibly competitive, especially if you’re new to NYC. A no-fee apartment means that renters won’t have to pay any fee that goes to the brokers. In NYC, any rental apartment that isn’t leased directly from the landlord or management company will require broker fees. Heritage Collection New York apartments are popular accommodation of choice for summer rentals. So if you have an internship programme in NYC this summer, Heritage Collection New York is where you need to be.

A no-fee apartment saves you a significant amount of money on your rent since most broker fees cost between 8 to 15% of a full year’s lease over the entire year. Furthermore, you don’t need to waste time hunting for reliable real estate agents or a trusted property manager.

Heritage Collection New York also provide flexible lease terms. This means that you can commit to a lease as short as 1 month\*. . Once you sign the lease, all you have to do is pack your bags and get ready to move-in.

_\*Terms and conditions apply._

## Frequently Asked Questions

##### What Makes a Serviced Apartment in Singapore?

+

Serviced apartments in Singapore usually offer many amenities typically found in hotels plus much more, such as fully equipped kitchens, multiple bedrooms, and even washer/dryer combos.

At Heritage Collection, our spacious apartments offer flexible lease terms (suitable for those looking for a short term stay), weekly housekeeping, complimentary laundry facilities, and a fully equipped kitchenette, with no hidden fees.

##### Are Serviced Apartments Cheaper than Hotels?

+

If you're looking for a place to stay for an extended period of time, serviced apartments are the smarter choice.

Generally speaking, they cost less than a hotel room and sometimes considerably less when staying over a long period of time. Not only are they more affordable but they provide a large number of features that you would expect from living at home rather than in a hotel room while still providing the same level of service.

Therefore, if you are considering your options on where to stay during an extended trip away, serviced apartments could be the right choice for you not only because it is cheaper than traditional hotels but also due to their diverse range of benefits for their guests.

If you are looking to stay for at least 3 consecutive months, Heritage Collection offers a greater discount on your monthly rental.

##### Where are the different Heritage Collection Serviced Apartments Located?

+

Heritage Collection's Serviced Apartments have a few locations islandwide:

- City Hall
- Robertson Quay
- Kreta Ayer
- Club Street
- Cecil Street

These Serviced Apartment Singapore locations are made to be convenient for our guests, and they are near many popular places, including the Central Business District, Arab Street, and Orchard Road. Nearby transport includes Clarke Quay, City Hall, Chinatown, and Somerset MRT station.

## Digital Aparthotel Experience
[Skip to content](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/#primary)

# Digital Aparthotel

## What To Expect For Your Stay

![Connect with us](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/digitalaparthotel.jpg)

# Your Heritage Collection Digital Experience

# Mobile Self Check-in

Our mobile self check-in system was introduced to provide our guests with a seamless, fully-digitalised check-in experience. Check-in at your own pace, you only need your smartphone with you.

3 days prior to your arrival, you will receive a reminder email from our app partner, Gtriip, with instructions for online registration. You will be required to provide a copy of your passport photo, submit your S$200 incidental deposit, as well as agree to our terms and conditions. Upon completing your online registration, you will receive your check-in instructions and pin code to enter the property. The faster check-in process gives you more time for adventures in the city.

* * *

# Digital Concierge

Our Digital Concierge Team will connect with you 1 day prior to your arrival to provide you with all the necessary information for your smooth check-in. At any point during your stay, they will also render remote assistance via Whatsapp for anything you may need during your stay. Whether it’s food recommendations or navigating through the city, our Digital Concierge can provide that assistance to you.

* * *

# Heritage Ambassadors

While we aim to provide you with a unique experience through technology, we understand that human interaction cannot be fully replaced by technology because of the connection it fosters. Our Heritage Ambassadors are the friendly faces that you may see during your stay with us. Although you may not physically see them on-site, they are available to assist you daily from 9:00AM to 6:00PM – just drop them a text, and they’ll be there!

* * *

# Amenities

Being a digital aparthotel doesn’t mean compromising on our guests’ stay experience.

Here’s what your Heritage collection experience entails:

- Complimentary shared laundry facilities (in selected locations only)
- Fully-equipped kitchenette with in-room cooking facilities such as the multi-purpose cooker
- Complimentary weekly housekeeping if you stay 7 nights and more
- B.Y.O.T (Bring Your Own Toiletries) – we believe in traveling sustainably and making conscious choices. We do not provide disposable bathroom amenities.

* * *

# FAQs

You can read more commonly asked questions about the brand [here.](https://hericoll.com/frequently-asked-questions/)

* * *

# Contact Information

If you have any more questions or if you are unsure about our policies, feel free to chat with us or connect with us via email at [sgenquiry@hericoll.com](mailto: sgenquiry@hericoll.com).

# Experience Heritage Living In A Shophouse Boutique Hotel and Serviced Apartment

Located within Singapore's cultural districts, we are the largest provider of short term and long term accommodation in these neighbourhoods. Our [boutique hotels](https://hericoll.com/blog/best-boutique-hotels-in-singapore/) and serviced apartments are stocked with the essentials needed for your stay, and you're always steps away from the best local food haunts and cultural attractions.

Choose your preferred location based on our Neighbourhoods, or browse by Room type.

## Heritage Collection FAQ
[Skip to content](https://hericoll.com/frequently-asked-questions/#primary)

# FAQ

Be mindful of classes here. This is a comment and it will not appear on the actual page.

# Property

# What is Heritage Collection?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Heritage Collection is a 3.5 stars accommodations provider located within Singapore’s Downtown Core. Our digital aparthotel are nestled within charming heritage shophouses in Singapore’s vibrant cultural neighbourhoods. Whether you’re in Singapore for short-term (less than 1 month), an extended stay (less than 3 months), or long-term (3 months and more), you’ll find something for your needs and budget. Check out our rooms if you’re keen on a shophouse-style living!

# What type of rooms are available at Heritage Collection properties?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Designed to encapsulate a shophouse-style living, we have a variety of room types suitable for all budget range – from a Studio Single (No Window) to a Loft (with private balcony), we got you covered! Head over [HERE](https://hericoll.com/collections/) to view what we have to offer.

# How much does a typical room cost at Heritage Collection?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Our room rates start from as low as SGD100 nett per night to SGD400 nett per night depending on the room category and time of the year. If your travel dates are all set, you may check out our rates & availability [HERE](http://booking.hericoll.com/).

# Where are Heritage Collection properties located?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

We are located within 3 of Singapore’s vibrant cultural neighbourhoods 1) Bugis, 2) Quays, and 3) Chinatown. Our addresses can be found within the rooms. Feel free to browse!

# What is Heritage Collection’s mobile self check-in procedure?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Your Heritage Collection experience is designed with your convenience in mind. As a digital aparthotel, check-in/check-out will be done via your smartphone and you will be required to download our mobile app. You will receive your pre-arrival instructions via email to complete your online registration, where you will be required to provide a copy of your passport photo, submit your S$200 incidental deposit, as well as agree to our terms and conditions. Upon completing your online registration, you will receive your check-in details and instructions.

Once you have arrive at the property on your arrival day, retrieve your digital key via the mobile app upon arrival. Don’t worry about long queues at the reception desk as check-in is done at your own convenience!

# What is the standard check-in & check-out time of Heritage Collection?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Check-in time is from 3:00PM, and check-out time is by 11:00AM. Early check-ins (before 3pm) and late check-outs (after 11am) are subject to availability and additional charges may apply. You may check with your Digital Concierge closer to your arrival or departure day.

# Will there be any incidental deposit required for my stay?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Yes. A S$200 incidental deposit is required and this will be in the form of an authorisation hold on your credit/debit card. Assuming that there are no missing items and/or damages in the room, the authorisation hold will be released at the end of your stay.

# Is the Front Desk available 24/7?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Heritage Collection is fully operated by a mobile self check-in system and we do not have a physical reception or front desk. At any point during your stay, our Digital Concierge team is available 24/7 to provide you with remote assistance via Whatsapp, if required.

# What is Heritage Collection’s smoking policy?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Smoking or any other related activity, which may result in a potential fire hazard, is not allowed in all Heritage Collection properties. A fine will be incurred if guests are found smoking, and shall be liable for all damages found associated with the smoking and/or related activities within the premises.

# I intend to travel with my family. Is that possible?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

We are happy to welcome your family if all of our hotel terms and conditions are met! For Single Rooms, the maximum occupancy per room is 1 guest only. For Studio and Loft Rooms, the maximum occupancy per room is 2 guests only. If you are travelling in a group, we require you to book multiple rooms according to the total number of pax. We also observe a no-child policy, and the minimum age to stay with us is 18 years old.

# Can I bring my four-legged furry friend and/or other pets?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

As much as we’d love to accommodate you and your pet(s), all Heritage Collection properties are not pet-friendly.

# Amenities

# What are the facilities available at Heritage Collection properties?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Our facilities include complimentary wifi, complimentary laundry facilities (in selected locations) for in-house guests, fully equipped kitchenette in all rooms, mobile self check-in system, and complimentary on-site luggage storage (from 9:00AM to 6:00PM daily). For a full list of in-room amenities, feel free to browse through our rooms!

# Do Heritage Collection properties have gym and swimming pool access?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

We do not have a gym and swimming pool in all our properties.

# Are there accessible rooms available?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Due to the nature of the shophouse designs and hotel architecture, we do not have accessible rooms.

# I am driving to one of your properties. Do you have on-site car parking facility?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

We do not have on-site parking facilities nor do we hold any reserved car park lots. Street parking are available nearby but lots are limited and public parking rates apply. You can check with us for more info on public carparks that are located close to the property that you’ve booked.

# Does Heritage Collection offer complimentary airport shuttle services?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

At this point of time, we do not offer complimentary airport shuttle service to guests. For transfers from Changi Airport, please locate the taxi signage upon your arrival and mention the chosen hotel address. Alternatively, public transport is easily available.

# Do you offer room service?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

We do not offer room service and dining options. That said, you may explore the neighbourhood and try out local delights.

# Is extra bed available?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Due to the unique nature of our shophouses, we are not able to accommodate extra beds in your room. Single rooms are fitted with 1 x single bed. Studio and Loft Rooms are fitted with either a king-sized or queen-sized bed. We recommend looking into additional rooms if your party exceeds the maximum number of guests per room.

# Bookings

# What is Heritage Collection’s cancellation policy?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

All bookings are strictly non-refundable, non-amendable and non-exchangeable. We highly recommend that your own suitable travel insurance is in place at the time of booking.

# Is there a minimum stay requirement?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Our minimum length of stay typically starts at 2 nights for selected hotels, and 6 nights for our service apartments. If your travel dates are all set, you may check out our rates & availability [HERE](http://booking.hericoll.com/). If you need further assistance, feel free to connect with us.

# Are the rates on your website per night or the total rates for the stay?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

The listed rates reflected are on a per night basis. Once you’ve confirmed and selected your preferred room, the total rates for your stay will be reflected. Rates are already inclusive of all taxes and fees.

# How do I pay for my booking?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

We only accept payments via credit/debit cards for bookings made on our Website. Your card will be charged automatically to confirm your booking. Our payment gateway is supported by [Stripe](https://stripe.com/en-sg), and you will be charged in Singapore Dollars (SGD).

# I have made a booking from your website. What do I do now?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

Awesome! Our team will be in touch with you via email to confirm your booking and to share your pre-arrival instructions via email. All information pertaining to your arrival experience will be in there. If you don’t see our email in your main inbox, it could be in your junk/spam folder.

# I had an excellent stay at Heritage Collection. How do I leave a review for the team?

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/faq-revamp-arrow.png)

You will receive a post-stay survey from us within 24 hours of your departure. We’d appreciate it if you can give a shout out to the team member that specifically moved you during your stay experience with us!

# Earn, Redeem, Repeat

## Elevate your Heritage Collection experience with our rewards program

![Connect with us](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/rewards-banner.jpg)

## Why join Heritage Rewards?

Whether you're a solo explorer or a duo seeking the next escapade, our rewards programme is sure to enhance your Heritage Collection experience.

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/earn-icon.svg)

# **Earn**

We appreciate you for making us your choice accommodation and we want to make every stay count.

**For every $1 you spend, we'll reward you with 1 point - it's that easy!**

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/redeem-icon.svg)

# **Redeem**

Enjoy great savings when you book more nights with us, and enjoy even greater savings when you pay with your points.

**Every stay gets sweeter with every point you redeem.**

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/repeat-icon.svg)

# **Repeat**

Continue unlocking the best rates and discounts only available to our members. We know what it means to keep your stays affordable.

**Come back and book more nights with us.**

# Duplex (Skylight)

City Hall

Nett Price from SGD 5,400/month

570 sq ft

4 person (min. 3 months stay)

Walking Distance to Bugis, Bras Basah, City Hall and Esplanade MRT

## Duplex (Skylight)

City Hall

Previous

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/city-hall-duplex-skylight/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/city-hall-duplex-skylight/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/25_InductionCooker.svg)

Kitchenette

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/28_LiftAccess.svg)

Lift Access

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/30_WasherDryer.svg)

In-Room Washer & Dryer

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/27_Wifi.svg)

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

The Duplex (Skylight) Apartment has a unique layout that has a mezzanine level for privacy and added space. A spiral staircase that lends a charming and heritage feel to the space, and separates the living space from the bedroom

- The apartment is furnished with a kitchen sink, Sofa Bed, and a Skylight window that cannot be opened
- Close proximity to landmarks such as CHIJMES, Suntec City, and Esplanade. Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 3 months is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

Heritage Apartments City Hall is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Enquire Today!

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Studio Premium Apartments are suitable for working professionals or couples looking for a larger space with in-unit laundry facilities. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this apartment will be your home away from home. The unit is furnished with a kitchen sink and an in-unit washer/dryer.

# Studio

City Hall

Nett Price from SGD 4,500/month

213 sq ft

2 person (min. 3 months stay)

Walking Distance to Bugis, Bras Basah, City Hall and Esplanade MRT

## Studio

City Hall

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/city-hall-studio/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/city-hall-studio/#)

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Studio Apartments are ideal for working professionals or couples looking for a cosy private space. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this apartment will be your home away from home. All you have to do is just pack your suitcases. There are varying layouts for our studio apartments, and actual unit allocated may differ in terms of configuration.

- Close proximity to landmarks such as CHIJMES, Suntec City, and Esplanade. Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 3 months is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

Heritage Apartments City Hall is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

# Duplex

City Hall

Nett Price from SGD 5,100/month

490 sq ft

2 person (min. 3 months stay)

Walking Distance to Bugis, Bras Basah, City Hall and Esplanade MRT

## Duplex

City Hall

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/city-hall-duplex/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/city-hall-duplex/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

Kitchenette

Lift Access

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/30_WasherDryer.svg)

In-Room Washer & Dryer

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/27_Wifi.svg)

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

The Duplex Apartment has a unique layout that has a mezzanine level for privacy and added space. The spiral staircase lends a charming and heritage feel to the space, separating the living space from the bedroom. The unit is furnished with a kitchen sink, a Sofa Bed, and an in-unit washer/dryer

- Close proximity to landmarks such as CHIJMES, Suntec City, and Esplanade. Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 3 months is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

Heritage Apartments City Hall is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Enquire Today!

## Premium Loft

227 South Bridge Road, Singapore 058776

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/chinatown-premium-loft/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/chinatown-premium-loft/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/51_WalkUp.svg)

Shophouse walk-up

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/30_WasherDryer.svg)

In-Room Washer & Dryer

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/50_Loft.svg)

Loft

Our Premium Loft has a unique layout that has a bedroom on an upper loft for added privacy. The high ceilings and large windows allow natural light to fill up the apartment to create an airy feel. Fully furnished with essential amenities, like your home away from home.

- Ideal for couples travelling together or business travelers looking for a larger space with in-room laundry facilities, especially for an extended stay
- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

## Premium Loft (Skylight)

227 South Bridge Road, Singapore 058776

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/227SB207.jpg)

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/chinatown-premium-loft-skylight/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/chinatown-premium-loft-skylight/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/51_WalkUp.svg)

Shophouse walk-up

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/13_Laundry.svg)

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/50_Loft.svg)

Loft

Our Premium Loft (Skylight) has a unique layout that has a bedroom on an upper loft for added privacy. Fully furnished with essential amenities, like your home away from home, and is ideal for couples travelling together or business travelers looking for a short stay

- The window in the apartment is a Skylight window that cannot be opened
- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Premium (No Window) Apartments are ideal for travellers looking for a larger space with in-room laundry facilities, especially for an extended stay. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home.

- This unit does not have windows. However, the interior of the apartment is brightly lit with lamps.
- The entrance to the apartment is located on the ground floor next to the famous street murals
- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

227 South Bridge Road, Singapore 058776

## Studio Single (Skylight)

227 South Bridge Road, Singapore 058776

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/chinatown-studio-single-skylight/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/chinatown-studio-single-skylight/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/51_WalkUp.svg)

Shophouse walk-up

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/13_Laundry.svg)

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Single (Skylight) Apartments are ideal for our solo travellers who spend most of the time exploring the city and want to come home to a fully furnished and self-contained accommodation.

- The window in the apartment is a Skylight window that cannot be opened.
- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/chinatown/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=chinatown&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

#### Key Features

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Loft Apartment offers guests with an added privacy with the bedroom on the upper loft. The high ceilings also create a spacious feel in the apartment, and the loft is also popular among those looking for a unique getaway. Equipped with the essential amenities required for your stay, this will be your home away from home.

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat
- Heritage Collection on Pagoda does not offer complimentary laundry facilities on-site. We’ve mapped out the coolest laundromats to add some excitement to your laundry day, feel free to ask us!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Pagoda is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

#### Key Features

Loft

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Loft (Skylight) Apartment offers guests with an added privacy with the bedroom on the upper loft. The high ceilings also create a spacious feel in the apartment, and the loft is also popular among those looking for a unique getaway. Equipped with the essential amenities required for your stay, this will be your home away from home.

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat
- This apartment is furnished with a Skylight window
- Heritage Collection on Pagoda does not offer complimentary laundry facilities on-site. We’ve mapped out the coolest laundromats to add some excitement to your laundry day, feel free to ask us!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Pagoda is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/chinatown/)

# Studio Single

63 Pagoda Street, Singapore 059222

Nett Price from SGD 135/night

118 sq ft

1 person

Walking distance to Chinatown MRT Station

## Studio Single

63 Pagoda Street, Singapore 059222

Previous

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/63P-SS-2.jpg)

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-single-p/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-single-p/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/51_WalkUp.svg)

Shophouse walk-up

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/25_InductionCooker.svg)

Kitchenette

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Single Apartment is ideal for solo travellers who spend most of the time exploring the city and want to come home to a fully furnished and self-contained accommodation.

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown – which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat

- Heritage Collection on Pagoda does not offer complimentary laundry facilities on-site. We’ve mapped out the coolest laundromats to add some excitement to your laundry day, feel free to ask us!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Pagoda is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/chinatown/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=pagoda&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Our Junior Studio Apartments are suitable for leisure or business travellers seeking a fully-furnished, self-contained accommodation. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home.

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat
- Selected unit does not come with a kitchen sink, and allocation of units will be strictly done by the Hotel.
- Heritage Collection on Pagoda does not offer complimentary laundry facilities on-site. We’ve mapped out the coolest laundromats to add some excitement to your laundry day, feel free to ask us!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Pagoda is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Deluxe Apartment offers a larger-sized apartment, suitable for those seeking for a comfortable full-furnished accommodation for an extended stay. Equipped with the essential amenities required for your stay, experience fuss-free living with Heritage Collection

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat
- Heritage Collection on Pagoda does not offer complimentary laundry facilities on-site. We’ve mapped out the coolest laundromats to add some excitement to your laundry day, feel free to ask us!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Pagoda is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Loft

Our Premium Loft Plus has a unique layout that has a bedroom on an upper loft for added privacy. The high ceilings coupled with large windows create an airy and spacious feel in the apartment.

- Close proximity to landmarks such as CHIJMES, Suntec City, and Esplanade
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- Height clearance of the loft is 1.6m

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Seah is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

Our Premium Loft is a chic, cosy apartment which provides you with the comfort you need for a much needed rest. The charming nature of our heritage shophouses means that selected units are furnished with a Skylight window that cannot be opened, and the apartment that you will be allocated may differ slightly in layout from the ones you see in our pictures. No two rooms are alike.

- Close proximity to landmarks such as CHIJMES, Suntec City, and Esplanade
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- Height clearance of the loft is 1.6m

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker

- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

# Loft

39 Seah Street, Singapore 188395

Nett price from SGD 97/night

155 sq ft

2 person

Walking Distance to Bugis, Bras Basah, City Hall and Esplanade MRT

## Loft

39 Seah Street, Singapore 188395

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/sh-loft/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/sh-loft/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/28_LiftAccess.svg)

Lift Access

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/13_Laundry.svg)

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/50_Loft.svg)

Loft

Our Loft has a minimal yet furnished interior with a comfortable queen-sized bed for you to come back to after a long day of exploring the city. The charming nature of our heritage shophouses means that selected units are furnished with a Skylight window that cannot be opened, and the apartment that you will be allocated may differ slightly in layout from the ones you see in our pictures. No two rooms are alike.

- Close proximity to landmarks such as CHIJMES, Suntec City, and Esplanade
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- Height clearance of the loft is 1.6m

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Seah is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Explore our rooms in 360°

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio (No Window) Apartments are perfect for our travellers who spend the day out and about and are looking for a comfortable apartment to come home to. Fully furnished with essential amenities, experience fuss-free living with Heritage Collection.

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat
- There are no windows in this apartment.
- Heritage Collection on Pagoda does not offer complimentary laundry facilities on-site. We’ve mapped out the coolest laundromats to add some excitement to your laundry day, feel free to ask us!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Pagoda is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

## Studio (No Window)

76 Boat Quay, Singapore 049864

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-no-window-qw/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-no-window-qw/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

Kitchenette

Shophouse walk-up

Queen-Sized Bed

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/27_Wifi.svg)

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Our Studio (No Window) Apartment is perfect for a quiet getaway from the buzzing city life.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your fuss-free stay-in.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Our Studio Plus (No Window) Apartment is perfect for those looking for a quiet getaway from the buzzing city life.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required, including an in-unit washer and dryer
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Our Studio Apartment offers guests a cosy space – ideal for those looking for a unique staycation to discover the neighbourhood, or business travelers in search for a home away from home.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- The safety and security of our guests are of utmost importance. Not all windows in this room category can be opened.

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). 2 person

Walking distance to Clarke Quay MRT Station

## Studio Plus

76 Boat Quay, Singapore 049864

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-plus-qw/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-plus-qw/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/25_InductionCooker.svg)

Kitchenette

Shophouse walk-up

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/30_WasherDryer.svg)

In-Room Washer & Dryer

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/27_Wifi.svg)

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Our Studio Plus Apartment provides guest with a cosy space – ideal for those looking for a unique staycation to explore the riverfront, or business travelers in search for a home away from home.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required, including an in-unit washer and dryer
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- The safety and security of our guests are of utmost importance. Not all windows in this room category can be opened.

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/the-quays/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=boatquay&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Our Studio Premium Apartment provides guests with a restful space after spending an adventurous day in the neighbourhood.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required, this will be your home away from home.
- Wake up to the picturesque view of the Singapore River, Marina Bay, and the Civic District.
- The safety and security of our guests are of utmost importance. Not all windows in this room category can be opened.

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Loft

Our Loft (No Window) Apartment offers guests an added privacy with the bedroom on the upper loft. The high ceilings also create a spacious feel in the apartment – perfect for quiet getaway from the buzzing city life.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/the-quays/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=boatquay&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Loft

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
