Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Our Studio Premium Apartments are ideal for travellers looking for a larger space with in-room laundry facilities, especially for an extended stay. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home.

- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

Our Studio Premium Plus Apartment provides guests with a restful space after spending an adventurous day in the neighbourhood.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required, including an in-unit washer and dryer.
- Wake up to the picturesque view of the Singapore River, Marina Bay, and the Civic District.
- The safety and security of our guests are of utmost importance. Not all windows in this room category can be opened.

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

#### Key Features

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Our Stamford Studio is thoughtfully furnished to provide our guests with a one-of-a-kind experience. It comes with a spacious private balcony for you to bask in the panoramic view of the Singapore River, Marina Bay and Civic District skylines. When evening comes, enjoy the ambience of the River, where history started for Singapore.

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Single Bed

Our Studio Single (No Window) Apartment is for solo travellers who prefer to spend their time out and about and only need accommodation to come home to after a day out in the city.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Arab is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-single-arab-street/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-single-arab-street/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/28_LiftAccess.svg)

Lift Access

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/13_Laundry.svg)

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/46_Single.svg)

Single Bed

Our Studio Single Apartment is the ideal unit for solo travellers who are always on the move for a daytime adventure, or those who want to enjoy a peaceful solitude any day of the week.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Arab is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio (No Window) Apartment is perfect for those looking for a unique and fuss-free working or study space. Wind down and get comfortable in our apartment after a long, hectic day. The interior of the apartment is brightly lit even though there are no windows in the unit.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Arab is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Weekly Housekeeping

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Apartment offers guests a cosy space for those who are looking for a unique staycation to discover the neighbourhood. The window in the apartment overlooks Arab Street’s vibrant back alley. You can also strike a pose or two with Yip Yew Chong’s famous mural on the alley.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Arab is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/50_Loft.svg)

Loft

Our Loft Single (No Window) allows you to experience attic living, with the bedroom on the upper loft. Tucked away on the highest level of the shophouse, you will get to enjoy peaceful vibes in the comfort of your own room. This unit is perfect for those looking for a quiet getaway from the buzzing city life.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- Height clearance of the loft is 2m

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Arab is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Our Studio Single (No Window) Apartments are ideal for independent urbanites looking for a fuss-free accommodation. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this apartment will be your home away from home.

- There are 2 interior layouts for these apartments, as shown in the photos. We cannot guarantee the room allocation as the availability of these units are subject to change.

Ann Siang is apopular and bustling area with many restaurants and bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Guests will experience street noise and nightlife musicfrom neighbouring bars. We do not recommendthis property to guests who do not enjoy nightlife music and are lightsleepers. We strongly advise guests toreconsiderthis neighbourhood prior to making a reservation.

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 6 nights is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/chinatown/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=annsiang&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Our Studio Apartments are suitable for working professionals or couples looking for a space that are move-in ready. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this apartment will be your home away from home.

Ann Siang is apopular and bustling area with many restaurants and bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Guests will experience street noise and nightlife musicfrom neighbouring bars. We do not recommendthis property to guests who do not enjoy nightlife music and are lightsleepers. We strongly advise guests toreconsiderthis neighbourhood prior to making a reservation.

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 6 nights is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

Our Studio Premium Apartments are ideal for couples travelling together or business travelers. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home. Clarke Quay is a blend of vibrant nightlife, heritage and business all in one place. Ideal for travellers looking for a larger space especially for an extended stay.

- Apartment also comes with a kitchen sink
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Clarke Quay is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

Our Studio Apartments are ideal for couples travelling together or business travelers looking for a short stay. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home. Clarke Quay is a blend of vibrant nightlife, heritage and business all in one place with food and grocery options only a stone’s throw away!

- Selected unit comes with a Skylight window that cannot be opened.
- Selected units are located on the 5th floor, and have to be accessed with a flight of stairs.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Clarke Quay is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

#### Key Features

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

Our Studio (No Window) Apartments are ideal for couples travelling together or business travelers looking for a short stay. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home. Clarke Quay is a blend of vibrant nightlife, heritage and business all in one place

- There are no windows in this apartment.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Clarke Quay is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/the-quays/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=clarkequay&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

Our Studio Single (No Window) Apartments are ideal for solo travelers looking for fuss-free accommodation. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home. Clarke Quay is a blend of vibrant nightlife, heritage and business all in one place

- There are no windows in this apartment.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Clarke Quay is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Single (No Windows) Apartments are ideal for our solo travellers who spend most of the time exploring the city and want to come home to a fully furnished and self-contained accommodation.

- There are 2 interior design styles for these apartments, as shown in the photos. We cannot guarantee the room allocation as the availability of these units are subject to change.
- There are no windows in these apartments. However the interior of the apartment is brightly lit with lamps.
- Located right in the heart of Chinatown – which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat

_Selected bookings may be eligible for complimentary upgrades, where available. This upgrade may be available at another Heritage Collection property within the same neighbourhood (~5 mins walk)._

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Our Studio (No Window) Apartments are ideal for couples travelling together or travelers looking for a short stay. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home.

- There are 2 interior design styles for these apartments, as shown in the photos. We cannot guarantee the room allocation as the availability of these units are subject to change.
- There are no windows in these apartments. However the interior of the apartment is brightly lit with lamps.
- Located right in the heart of Chinatown, which means that there are endless things to see, do and eat.

_Selected bookings may be eligible for complimentary upgrades, where available. This upgrade may be available at another Heritage Collection property within the same neighbourhood (~5 mins walk)._

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Chinatown is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Our Studio Premium Apartments are ideal for couples travelling together or business travellers looking for a flexible, extended stay option. The unit boasts a scenic view of the Singapore River. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home.

Boat Quay is a popular and bustling area with many restaurants and bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Guests will experience street noise and nightlife music from neighbouring bars. We do not recommend this property to guests who do not enjoy nightlife music and are light sleepers. We strongly advise guests to reconsider this neighbourhood prior to making a reservation.

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 6 nights is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (South Bridge Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

#### Key Features

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Studio Single Apartments are ideal for solo travellers looking for fuss-free extended stay accommodation. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this will be your home away from home.

Boat Quay is a popular and bustling area with many restaurants and bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Guests will experience street noise and nightlife music from neighbouring bars. We do not recommend this property to guests who do not enjoy nightlife music and are light sleepers. We strongly advise guests to reconsider this neighbourhood prior to making a reservation.

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 6 nights is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (South Bridge Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/the-quays/)

#### Key Features

Our Studio Single (No Window) Apartment is for solo travellers who prefer to spend their time out and about and only need accommodation to come home to after a day out in the city.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your fuss-free stay-in.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/50_Loft.svg)

Loft

Our Raffles Loft has a private balcony, providing you with a scenic view of the Singapore River, Marina Bay and Civic District skylines. The high ceiling in our loft creates a spacious feel, perfect for you to enjoy the day & night ambience of the River, where history started for Singapore.

Tip: The neighbourhood is bustling with restaurants/bars in the vicinity which operate into the night. Although our rooms are soundproofed, guests may still experience some street noise and light sleepers are advised to reconsider prior to making a reservation.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Boat Quay (Quayside Wing) is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

Explore our rooms in 360°

Our Studio Apartments are ideal for working professionals or couples looking for a cosy private space. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this apartment will be your home away from home. All you have to do is just pack your suitcases.

- With the Chinatown food street at your doorstep, be prepared to transform your stomach into a bottomless pit

* * *

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 6 nights is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

Kitchenette

Queen-Sized Bed

24 Hour CCTV Protection

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/27_Wifi.svg)

Complimentary High Speed Wifi

Our Studio Premium Apartments are suitable for working professionals or couples looking for a larger space with in-unit laundry facilities. Fully furnished with essential amenities, this apartment will be your home away from home. This unit is furnished with a kitchen sink and an in-unit washer/dryer

- Located at the doorstep of Singapore’s Central Business District, surrounded by buildings like Cecil Court, Asia Square and CapitaGreen. Surrounded by restaurants and bars in the vicinity, offering a variety of F&B options

* * *

- Minimum stay of 6 nights is required
- Rates include weekly housekeeping and utilities

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/business-district/)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

# Garden Studio Deluxe

133 Jln Sultan, Singapore 199016

Nett price from SGD 160/night

157 sq ft

2 person

Lavender MRT Station

## Garden Studio Deluxe

133 Jln Sultan, Singapore 199016

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/garden-studio-deluxe/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/garden-studio-deluxe/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/51_WalkUp.svg)

Shophouse walk-up

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/15_Housekeeping.svg)

Weekly Housekeeping

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/13_Laundry.svg)

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Step into your own private oasis in our Garden Studio Deluxe. Whether it’s for unwinding or engage in meaningful conversations with your partner, guests can enjoy the outdoors comfortably in their private garden. It is perfect for travellers seeking a peaceful and rejuvenating getaway.

- Unit is located on the ground floor; guests are required to walk-up to Level 2 to access the complimentary shared laundry facilities
- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

Guests are reminded to keep the doors to their garden locked at all times if not in used. Heritage Collection will not be responsible for any missing or damaged items due to guests’ own negligence during their stay.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/bugis-district/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=victoria&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Step into your own private oasis in our Garden Studio Queen Room. Whether it’s for unwinding or engage in meaningful conversations with your partner, guests can enjoy the outdoors comfortably in their private garden. It is perfect for travellers seeking a peaceful and rejuvenating getaway.

- Unit is located on the ground floor; guests are required to walk-up to Level 2 to access the complimentary shared laundry facilities
- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

Guests are reminded to keep the doors to their garden locked at all times if not in used. Heritage Collection will not be responsible for any missing or damaged items due to guests’ own negligence during their stay.

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). #### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/loft-deluxe/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/loft-deluxe/#)

Mobile Self Check-In

Loft

Experience elevated comfort in our Loft Deluxe Apartment. This stylishly designed space offers a more spacious two-level layout, maximising space and creating a sense of openness. The upper loft area features a queen-sized bed, promising a restful night’s sleep.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- Height clearance of the loft is 1.2M

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/bugis-district-2/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=victoria&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

#### Key Features

[View All Amenities](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-queen-no-window/#) [View All](https://hericoll.com/collections/studio-queen-no-window/#)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/60_Mobile_Check_In.svg)

Mobile Self Check-In

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/51_WalkUp.svg)

Complimentary Laundry Facilities

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/52_NoVanity.svg)

B.Y.O.T. (Bring Your Own Toiletries)

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/themes/heritage/img/39_QueenBed.svg)

Queen-Sized Bed

Our Studio Queen (No Window) Apartment offers guests a cosy space for those who are looking for a unique stay to discover the neighbourhood. There are no windows in this unit but the room is well-lit.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/bugis-district/)

[BOOK NOW](https://booking.hericoll.com/?location=victoria&checkindt=2025-06-17&checkoutdt=2025-06-18&guests=1)

Images taken are of actual apartments and may feature bed runners and cushions that are used for styling purposes. These items are not the standard amenities in all rooms and actual units allocated may differ slightly in terms of configuration.

Our Loft (No Window) Apartment offers guests an added privacy with the bedroom on the upper loft. There are no windows in this unit but the apartment is well-lit, and is also perfect for quiet getaway from the buzzing city life.

- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!
- Height clearance of the loft is 1.2M

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Kitchenette

Our Studio Double (No Window) Apartment offers guests a cosy space for those who are looking for a unique stay to discover the neighbourhood. Although there are no windows in this unit, the interior of the room is well-lit.

- This unit is furnished with a Double Bed (1.35m (W) x 1.9m (L)).
- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away!

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/bugis-district/)

Tatami Bed

Kitchenette

Escape to your own tranquil oasis in this beautifully designed Loft Tatami (No Window) room room. Tucked away on the highest level of the shophouse, you will get to enjoy peaceful vibes in the comfort of your own room – perfect for unwinding after a long day. This unit is perfect for those looking for a quiet getaway from the hustle & bustle of city life.

- This room is equipped with a Tatami mattress
- This is an XXS room and the height clearance of the loft is between 0.7m and 1.2m.
- Fully furnished with the essential amenities required for your stay.
- Food and grocery options are also only a stone’s throw away

* * *

Each room is fitted with a kitchenette that comes with a microwave oven and water kettle. The following room amenities are available upon request and availability:

- Iron & Ironing Board
- Multi-Purpose Cooker
- Cooking Utensils
- Cork Pot Mat
- Knife & Chopping Board Set

* * *

Heritage Collection on Victoria is a digital aparthotel and access to your room is via a mobile key. To successfully retrieve the mobile key to your room, you’ll need to download our mobile app, complete your online registration, and provide a valid credit/debit card details for the S$200 incidental deposit hold. The credit/debit card must belong to the staying guest. Do keep an eye out for our email for more details after booking with us.

You may also watch our self check-in guide [video](https://www.canva.com/design/DAGFRiDEboQ/rDp0JwqnFyamVUjz51OZ8Q/watch?utm_content=DAGFRiDEboQ&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link&utm_source=editor). Find out more about your Heritage Collection Digital experience [here](https://hericoll.com/digital-aparthotel/).

[Explore Neighbourhoods](https://hericoll.com/bugis-district/)

## Heritage Shophouse Staycation
[Skip to content](https://hericoll.com/for-a-unique-staycation-consider-a-heritage-shophouse-an-article-by-silverkris/#primary)

[Back to all](https://hericoll.com/for-a-unique-staycation-consider-a-heritage-shophouse-an-article-by-silverkris/#)

# For a unique staycation, consider a heritage shophouse – an article by SilverKris

[Share](https://hericoll.com/for-a-unique-staycation-consider-a-heritage-shophouse-an-article-by-silverkris/#)

[Facebook](https://hericoll.com/#facebook "Facebook") [Twitter](https://hericoll.com/#twitter "Twitter") [Email](https://hericoll.com/#email "Email") [Share](https://www.addtoany.com/share#url=https%3A%2F%2Fhericoll.com%2Ffor-a-unique-staycation-consider-a-heritage-shophouse-an-article-by-silverkris%2F&title=For%20a%20unique%20staycation%2C%20consider%20a%20heritage%20shophouse%20%E2%80%93%20an%20article%20by%20SilverKris)

Press

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/09/image-1.png)

Conservation and adaptive reuse have been perennial buzzwords in Singapore’s urban-planning discourse. They entered common usage in recent years, thanks to big-scale public projects such as Victoria Theatre and National Gallery Singapore, involving big-name designers and architects. But the adaptive reuse of the city’s hundreds of smaller buildings and shophouses, though flying under the radar, is just as noteworthy and far more prevalent.

And now, with the arrival of our newest buzzword – staycation – Singaporean residents have a chance to appreciate these shophouses while being tourists in their hometown. [Heritage Collection](https://hericoll.com/) is a homegrown hospitality brand founded in 2011 with the aim to “marry the concepts of enterprise and conservation to deliver a unique experience that stands out from mainstream hotels,” says a spokesperson for the company.

Read the full article on SilverKris’ page here: [https://www.silverkris.com/for-a-unique-staycation-consider-a-heritage-shophouse/](https://www.silverkris.com/for-a-unique-staycation-consider-a-heritage-shophouse/)

# Lianhe Zaobao: 学校假期加上将分发旅游消费券 酒店纷推配套抢攻年底度宅假市场

[Share](https://hericoll.com/%e5%ad%a6%e6%a0%a1%e5%81%87%e6%9c%9f%e5%8a%a0%e4%b8%8a%e5%b0%86%e5%88%86%e5%8f%91%e6%97%85%e6%b8%b8%e6%b6%88%e8%b4%b9%e5%88%b8-%e9%85%92%e5%ba%97%e7%ba%b7%e6%8e%a8%e9%85%8d%e5%a5%97%e6%8a%a2%e6%94%bb/#)

[Facebook](https://hericoll.com/#facebook "Facebook") [Twitter](https://hericoll.com/#twitter "Twitter") [Email](https://hericoll.com/#email "Email") [Share](https://www.addtoany.com/share#url=https%3A%2F%2Fhericoll.com%2F%25e5%25ad%25a6%25e6%25a0%25a1%25e5%2581%2587%25e6%259c%259f%25e5%258a%25a0%25e4%25b8%258a%25e5%25b0%2586%25e5%2588%2586%25e5%258f%2591%25e6%2597%2585%25e6%25b8%25b8%25e6%25b6%2588%25e8%25b4%25b9%25e5%2588%25b8-%25e9%2585%2592%25e5%25ba%2597%25e7%25ba%25b7%25e6%258e%25a8%25e9%2585%258d%25e5%25a5%2597%25e6%258a%25a2%25e6%2594%25bb%2F&title=Lianhe%20Zaobao%3A%20%E5%AD%A6%E6%A0%A1%E5%81%87%E6%9C%9F%E5%8A%A0%E4%B8%8A%E5%B0%86%E5%88%86%E5%8F%91%E6%97%85%E6%B8%B8%E6%B6%88%E8%B4%B9%E5%88%B8%20%E9%85%92%E5%BA%97%E7%BA%B7%E6%8E%A8%E9%85%8D%E5%A5%97%E6%8A%A2%E6%94%BB%E5%B9%B4%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A6%E5%AE%85%E5%81%87%E5%B8%82%E5%9C%BA)

Press

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/98104-1024x499.jpg)

年底学校假期以往都是出国旅游的旺季，现在多数国人无法踏出国门，向本地酒店预订度宅假的生意也就看涨。酒店更是为即将推出的“重新探索新加坡”旅游消费券摩拳擦掌，纷纷推出特别配套准备吸引引国人人住。

## Best Serviced Apartments

# Best In Singapore: THE 6 BEST SERVICED APARTMENTS IN SINGAPORE

[Share](https://hericoll.com/best-in-singapore-the-6-best-serviced-apartments-in-singapore/#)

[Facebook](https://hericoll.com/#facebook "Facebook") [Twitter](https://hericoll.com/#twitter "Twitter") [Email](https://hericoll.com/#email "Email") [Share](https://www.addtoany.com/share#url=https%3A%2F%2Fhericoll.com%2Fbest-in-singapore-the-6-best-serviced-apartments-in-singapore%2F&title=Best%20In%20Singapore%3A%20THE%206%20BEST%20SERVICED%20APARTMENTS%20IN%20SINGAPORE)

Press

If you’re looking for a temporary place to live in — whether because you’re still seeking permanent lodgings or because you’re only in the country temporarily — serviced apartments offer a nice option. With a serviced apartment, you get the perks of both private apartment living and hotel stays!

Fortunately, serviced apartments have been getting more popular in Singapore over the years. As such, there are now a lot of choices available for interested lodgers.

We took a look at the highest-rated serviced apartment providers in Singapore and compared them to each other. Afterwards, we ended up with a list of the best ones, which we’ll show you below:

### 5\. Heritage Collection

![](https://hericoll.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/03/1-2048x1536.jpg)

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **BEST FOR** | Culturally-interesting architecture and modern amenities |
| **SERVICES** | [https://hericoll.com/rooms/](https://hericoll.com/rooms/) |
| **WEBSITE** | [https://hericoll.com/](https://hericoll.com/) |
| **ADDRESS** | 227 South Bridge Road, Singapore 058776 |
| **CONTACT DETAILS** | +65 6223 7155 |

Unlike some other serviced apartments, Heritage Collection has corporate bookings for those who will be going on a business trip with a number of employees.

This would also be a great option for first-time visitors because the overall interior of the building has local architectural influences.

You also be able to visit the famous tourist attractions easily when staying here, as the apartments are located in the Central Business District.

#### Service Highlights

- Flexible lease terms with minimum stay of 3 months is required
- Fully furnished apartments
- Exclusively curated apartments and personalised services

#### **Customer Reviews**

Heritage Collection, previously known as Heritage Apartments, is known for their hospitality and attentive staff service. They consistently check up on their guests and provide for their needs according to their preferences.

A reviewer from Agoda praised the accommodations and shared this experience:

_“The City Hall Premium Loft in which my husband and I stayed for a week was a gorgeous, compact and well-planned space right in the heart of town. Considering my stay was for a week, it would have been great if towels were replaced once every couple days instead of having to use the same ones through the week. Towels aside, it was a comfortable stay and my Guest Relations Managers, Aryanna and Ray, really complemented my stay with their attentive service. Thank you Heritage Apartments for the wonderful week!”_

Another reviewer said:

_“It is difficult to describe the experience as it’s rare to be on receiving end of such above and beyond hospitality, especially in Singapore where for every 10 people one meets, maybe only 1 will be willing to make your stay a memorable one. But here at Heritage, the first person you meet, you Guest Experience (Aptly named) Manager, is clearly on a mission to make sure you remember Heritage and are willing to take the time, like what I am doing now, to write about your experience. The Loft unit i took was clean and the cutleries were spotless. The bed above was cozy and romantic and the TV has Netflix, Apple and what not to ensure you get the movie you want, not even Marina Bay Sands does this! MBS’s TV is not even Smart!_

_Just below the apartment, right outside the door in fact, conveniently located, is a Tapas bar that is full or cool people throughout the day. I thoroughly enjoyed my experience and I will definitely come back again to try another location, mine was on Seah. Lastly, a very special mention to my Guest Experience Manager Jarwin. He is very prompt and quietly efficient in going about his Job. I was completely taken by surprised when Jarwin went, I am not sure where, and bought for me a large packet of Ice when I told him I did not see a ice tray in the Fridge. I was expecting a tray and was given a packet! This is what I meant by going above and beyond. I will strongly recommend Heritage to everyone who is looking for an affordable yet exceptional experience. It is the kind of experience that one usually sees in cities like Prague and Berlin and seldom expected to see in Singapore. Kudos Heritage, I hope you guys have surpassed your own expectations :)))”_

Read full article [here](https://www.bestinsingapore.co/best-serviced-apartments-singapore/#5_Heritage_Collection).
